# Rhino Lapidary Shopify Files Content Audit

**Snapshot:** 2026-08-20  
**Store:** Rhino Lapidary  
**Scope:** Every item returned by the live Shopify Admin `files` connection, fully paginated.

## Executive summary

| Metric | Result |
|---|---:|
| Total files | 867 |
| Images | 793 — 1.06 GiB (1,140,168,771 B) |
| Shopify hosted videos | 37 — 7.86 GiB (8,434,384,766 B) — 53:49 (3,228,640 ms) total |
| Documents and generic files | 37 — 93.22 MiB (97,749,491 B) |
| Total original bytes | 9.01 GiB (9,672,303,028 B) |
| READY with no file errors | 867 |
| Missing alt text | 867 |
| Directly associated with product media | 95 |
| Not directly associated with product media | 772 |

## Development findings

- Every File object is `READY`, and Shopify reports no processing errors.
- Every asset has blank alt text. Product imagery therefore lacks usable alternative text at the Files layer; image alt governance should be added before these assets are reused in storefront sections.
- Video dominates storage: more than 8.4 GB decimal. Several videos exceed 500 MB, and three exceed 700 MB. Use posters, deferred loading, short previews, and selective publication rather than eager embedding.
- Only 95 files are directly associated with current `product.media`. The remaining 772 can still be referenced by theme settings, pages, rich text, metafields, or apps, so product detachment is not proof that deletion is safe.
- The supplied theme source snapshot contains no direct `shopify://shop_images/...` or Rhino Files CDN references. This does not cover Admin-authored page content, metafields, app content, or unpublished theme settings.
- Exact document duplicates were detected by SHA-256. Image duplicate groups are based on identical normalized 220 px Shopify CDN renditions. Video preview groups identify identical posters; only entries also sharing source size, duration, and dimensions are probable full video duplicates.
- Original byte sizes come from Shopify `MediaImage.originalSource.fileSize`, `Video.originalSource.fileSize`, or `GenericFile.originalFileSize`. URLs are the live Shopify hosted URLs returned by Admin GraphQL.

## Duplicate candidates

- Exact document duplicates: `LAPMASTER_12.pdf` / `LAPMASTER_12_LAPPING_MACHINE_12_PART_LIST.pdf`; `LAPMASTER_18_EXPLODED_PICTURES.pdf` / `LAPMASTER_18.pdf`; `BEADMASTER_EXPLODED_PICTURE..pdf` / `BEADMASTER.pdf`; `TRIMMASTER.pdf` / `TRIMMASTER_EXPLODED_PICTURES.pdf`.
- Probable full video duplicates: video rows 3 / 4 and 17 / 18 share the same preview, source byte size, duration, and dimensions. Hash the original streams before deletion.
- Identical image rendition groups are marked directly in the image table. Some are intentional reuse across mirrored or related SKUs, such as left and right arbor parts.

## Safe follow-up sequence

1. Add descriptive alt text to published product and homepage media first.
2. Build a reference crawler across theme JSON, page and article HTML, metafields, metaobjects, product descriptions, and app owned content before deleting detached Files.
3. Hash suspected duplicate originals, select a canonical asset, rewrite every reference, and only then remove duplicates.
4. Transcode oversized customer facing videos, preserve a master outside Shopify, and publish only web delivery variants.
5. Move internal meeting notes, dealer lists, production source files, and obsolete pamphlet drafts out of public Shopify Files if no storefront dependency exists.
